| `correlation_induce_univ` | `correlation (G.induce Set.univ) p A = correlation G p (A.map (Equiv.Set.univ V))` — since `G.induce Set.univ ≃g G` (mathlib `induceUnivIso`), pushing the observable along the relabeling preserves the correlation. Connects `inducedGraph _ univ` statements (the left side of `correlation_inducedGraph_deleteEdges_union_inl`) back to the raw graph `G` (the form of `correlation_sub_deleteEdges_le_derivBound`) for the per-stage increment assembly (former Issue #2965, Phase A) | `InducedUnion.lean` | Component split (former Issue #2965) |

Applies to `s = ↑(S ∪ Sᶜ)` (full by `Finset.union_compl`), letting the capstone's `inducedGraph _ (S ∪ Sᶜ)` left side connect to the raw bond-deleted graph **without** forcing the propositional `S ∪ Sᶜ = univ` at the type level — the clean route through the per-stage-increment type-transport (former Issue #2965, Phase A) | `InducedUnion.lean` | Component split (former Issue #2965) |

Bridges `Finset.Subtype.fintype` (from `inducedGraph`) vs the `Set`-induce vertex `Fintype` | `InducedUnion.lean` | Component split (former Issue #2965) |

Composes `correlation_induce_of_forall_mem` (on `↑(S∪Sᶜ)`) ∘ `correlation_congr_all` ∘ `correlation_inducedGraph_deleteEdges_union_inl`. Pairs with the ball-boundary increment `correlation_sub_deleteEdges_le_derivBound` (#2974) to give the numeric per-stage increment `correlation_pair_sub_inducedGraph_le_derivBound` (#2989) | `InducedUnion.lean` | Component split (former Issue #2965) |

Identifies the observable of `correlation_deleteEdges_straddle_eq_inducedGraph` with the raw `V`-vertex observable (e.g. `{r,s}` for the pair), matching `correlation_sub_deleteEdges_le_derivBound` (former Issue #2965, Phase A) | `InducedUnion.lean` | Component split (former Issue #2965) |

Composes the ball-boundary bond-deletion increment `correlation_sub_deleteEdges_le_derivBound` (#2974) with the component-factorization bridge `correlation_deleteEdges_straddle_eq_inducedGraph` (#2986) via `deleteEdges_filter_edgeFinset_eq` (#2987) + `correlation_congr_all` + `triple_map_subtypeUnivEquiv_eq` (#2988). The full-model pair correlation exceeds the isolated induced-subgraph pair correlation by at most the boundary `derivBound` over the cut edges | `PerStageIncrement.lean` | Per-stage increment (former Issue #2965) |
| `nestedSubtypeEquiv` / `inducedGraph_induce_preimage_map_eq` | For `S ⊆ T`, `((inducedGraph G T).induce {x : ↥T \| x.val ∈ S}).map nestedSubtypeEquiv = inducedGraph G S` — the nested induced subgraph (induce `box_k` inside `inducedGraph G box_{k+1}`) equals, up to the nested-subtype relabeling `{x : ↥T // x.val ∈ S} ≃ ↥S`, the direct induced subgraph on `S`. Graph-level foundation for instantiating the per-stage increment on cubic exhaustion stages `box_k ⊆ box_{k+1}` | `InducedUnion.lean` | Nested induce (former Issue #2965) |
| `correlation_inducedGraph_induce_preimage` | Correlation companion of the above: `correlation ((inducedGraph G T).induce {x \| x.val ∈ S}) p (A.map nestedSubtypeEquiv.symm) = correlation (inducedGraph G S) p A`. Proved by applying `correlation_map_equiv` to the *direct* `inducedGraph G S` with `nestedSubtypeEquiv.symm` (so the heavy nested-subtype graph is only the map *result*, never the graph `correlation_map_equiv` operates on — bypassing the `whnf` wall), then bridging via `correlation_congr_all` + the graph equality. The `c_k`-side identity for the cubic-exhaustion per-stage increment (former Issue #2965) | `InducedUnion.lean` | Nested induce (former Issue #2965) |
| `nestedFinsetEquiv` / `correlation_inducedGraph_nested_finset` | **Double-induce correlation identification (Finset route).** For `T₁ ⊆ T₂`, `correlation (inducedGraph (inducedGraph G T₂) (T₁.subtype (· ∈ T₂))) p (A.map nestedFinsetEquiv.symm) = correlation (inducedGraph G T₁) p A`, where `nestedFinsetEquiv : ↥(T₁.subtype (· ∈ T₂)) ≃ ↥T₁`. The concrete-layer companion of `correlation_inducedGraph_induce_preimage` (#2991), using the `Finset.subtype` slice instead of a `Set`-predicate so it matches the `S : Finset ↥T₂` of the single-box increment directly (avoiding the `↥S` vs `{x // x.val ∈ T₁}` type mismatch). Same `correlation_map_equiv`-on-the-direct-graph proof scheme | `PerStageIncrement.lean` | Nested induce (former Issue #2965) |
| `correlation_pair_two_box_le_derivBound` | **Two-box per-stage correlation increment (Phase A).** For nested finsets `T₁ ⊆ T₂`, `r,s ∈ T₁`, `r ≠ s`, ferromagnetic, `h=0`, and `r,s` not on any cut edge of the `T₁`-slice: `correlation (inducedGraph G T₂) p {⟨r,_⟩,⟨s,_⟩} − correlation (inducedGraph G T₁) p {⟨r,_⟩,⟨s,_⟩} ≤ derivBound (inducedGraph G T₂) (filter straddle) p ⟨r,_⟩ ⟨s,_⟩`. Composes the single-box increment `correlation_pair_sub_inducedGraph_le_derivBound` (#2989) on `G' = inducedGraph G T₂` with the double-induce identification `correlation_inducedGraph_nested_finset`, recovering `inducedGraph G T₁` on the inner box. The form instantiated on cubic exhaustion stages `box_k ⊆ box_{k+1}` to bound `c_{k+1} − c_k` | `PerStageIncrement.lean` | Per-stage increment (former Issue #2965) |
| `correlationAlongExhaustion_cubic_succ_sub_le_derivBound` | **Cubic-exhaustion per-stage increment (Phase A).** For the cubic exhaustion of `latticeGraph d` and a pair `r, s` interior to `box_k` (no cut edge of the `box_k`-slice): `correlationAlongExhaustion (latticeGraph d) (cubicExhaustion d) p {r,s} (k+1) − correlationAlongExhaustion … k ≤ derivBound (inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) box_{k+1}) (filter straddle) p ⟨r,_⟩ ⟨s,_⟩`. Instantiates the abstract two-box increment `correlation_pair_two_box_le_derivBound` with `T₁ = box_k ⊆ T₂ = box_{k+1}` (`cubicBox_mono`), unfolding `correlationAlongExhaustion` on both stages (both observable subsets hold by `cubicBox_mono`) and matching lifted observables via `liftFinset_pair`; edge-set `Fintype` hypotheses are discharged from finiteness of the box subtype. The correlation-side `c_{k+1} − c_k` bound, one input toward the volume-convergence rate program of GJ Lemma 17.5.2 (distinct from the β-derivative provider that program also requires) | `CubicPerStageIncrement.lean` | Per-stage increment (former Issue #2965) |
| `derivBound_inducedGraph_cubic_le_infiniteVolume_sum` (+ `correlation_inducedGraph_cubic_le_correlationInfinite`) | **Cubic-shell `derivBound` bounded by an infinite-volume two-point sum (Phase A→B link).** For `r,s ∈ box_n` and any edge set `E₀` of `inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) box_n`: `derivBound … E₀ … ⟨r,_⟩ ⟨s,_⟩ ≤ β·J·∑_{⟨a,b⟩∈E₀}[g{r,a}g{s,b} + g{r,b}g{s,a} + g{r,s}g{a,b}]`, where `g{x,y} = correlationInfinite (latticeGraph d) (cubicExhaustion d) p {x,y}` (the infinite-volume pair-set correlation; a two-point function for distinct sites, collapsing to a one-site correlation when `x = y`). Instantiates `derivBound_le_of_correlation_le` (#2994) with the infinite-volume bound `c α β = g{α.val,β.val}` (symmetric by `Finset.pair_comm`, nonneg by `correlationInfinite_nonneg`, dominating finite-volume correlations by the new finite ≤ infinite bridge `correlation_inducedGraph_cubic_le_correlationInfinite`, itself from `correlationAlongExhaustion_le_correlationInfinite_latticeGraph` + `liftFinset_pair`). Combined with the per-stage increment #2993, gives `c_{k+1} − c_k` bounded by an infinite-volume boundary sum — the input to the Phase B spatial-decay/summability results (#2966), with the divergent-prefactor-sensitive analysis isolated downstream | `CubicShellInfiniteVolumeBound.lean` | Per-stage increment (former Issue #2965) |
| **Tight per-stage increment chain** (`…_derivBoundTight` analogues) | **Diagonal-free (summable) per-stage chain (Phase A→B).** Tight analogues of the whole increment chain using `derivBoundTight` (cross products `⟨σ_rσ_k⟩⟨σ_sσ_l⟩ + ⟨σ_rσ_l⟩⟨σ_sσ_k⟩` only, **no** diagonal `⟨σ_rσ_s⟩⟨σ_kσ_l⟩` term — the latter would sum to `⟨σ_rσ_s⟩·O(\|shell\|)` and diverge). Built via the tight ball-boundary Simon–Lieb (`ball_boundary_simon_lieb_tight`, `cor_4_3_3_scaled`): `scaledCorrelation_one_sub_zero_le_derivBoundTight` → `correlation_sub_deleteEdges_le_derivBoundTight` (`Tight.lean`) → `correlation_pair_sub_inducedGraph_le_derivBoundTight` → `correlation_pair_two_box_le_derivBoundTight` (`PerStageIncrement.lean`) → `correlationAlongExhaustion_cubic_succ_sub_le_derivBoundTight` (`CubicPerStageIncrement.lean`), and `derivBoundTight_le_of_correlation_le` (`Tight.lean`) → `derivBoundTight_inducedGraph_cubic_le_infiniteVolume_sum` (`CubicShellInfiniteVolumeBound.lean`). Net: `c_{k+1} − c_k ≤ β·J·∑_{⟨a,b⟩∈shell}[g{r,a}g{s,b} + g{r,b}g{s,a}]` (g = infinite-volume correlation), the diagonal-free summable form on which Phase B spatial decay yields a geometric rate | `Tight.lean`, `PerStageIncrement.lean`, `CubicPerStageIncrement.lean`, `CubicShellInfiniteVolumeBound.lean` | Per-stage increment (former Issue #2965) |
| `derivBoundTight_inducedGraph_cubic_le_decay_sum` | **Cubic-shell tight bound by a spatial-decay sum (Phase B).** For `d ≥ 1`, ferromagnetic zero-field parameters (`h = 0`), in the high-temperature regime `contractionFactor d (cubicExhaustion d) p r₀ < 1`, and `r,s ∈ box_n` on no `E₀`-edge: `derivBoundTight … E₀ … ≤ β·J·∑_{⟨a,b⟩∈E₀}[cf^{d(r,a)/(r₀+2)}·cf^{d(s,b)/(r₀+2)} + cf^{d(r,b)/(r₀+2)}·cf^{d(s,a)/(r₀+2)}]` (cf = `contractionFactor`). Applies the per-pair spatial decay `correlationInfinite_latticeGraph_le_contractionFactor_pow_dist_pair` termwise to the diagonal-free infinite-volume bound `derivBoundTight_inducedGraph_cubic_le_infiniteVolume_sum` (distinctness `r ≠ a.val` etc. from the `E₀`-edge separation). The cross-product-only structure ensures every factor genuinely decays — the diagonal-free decay-sum input from which a geometric per-stage rate is extracted (shell-edge distance/counting aggregation downstream) | `CubicShellDecaySum.lean` (declaration-free facade; the declarations live in `CubicShellDecaySum/{ShellDecaySumBound,CorrelationIncrementPolyPow,SeparationHypothesis,GeometricUniformBounds}.lean`) | Per-stage increment (former Issue #2965) |
| `derivBoundTight_cubic_shell_le_card_pow` (+ `cf_pow_fresh_le`, `straddle_fresh_vertex`) | **Geometric per-stage bound (Phase B).** For `d ≥ 1`, ferromagnetic `h=0`, high temperature (`cf < 1`), and `r,s ∈ box_R` (`R ≤ k`) on no cut edge: `derivBoundTight (inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) box_{k+1}) shell … ≤ β·J·(shell.card • (2·cf^{(k+1−R)/(r₀+2)}))`. Each straddle edge of the `box_k`-slice has a fresh endpoint `∈ box_{k+1}\box_k` (`straddle_fresh_vertex`), whose distance to `r`/`s` is `≥ k+1−R` (`latticeDistance_ge_of_mem_cubicBox_succ_not_mem`), so its decay factor is `≤ cf^{(k+1−R)/(r₀+2)}` (`cf_pow_fresh_le`, `pow_le_pow_of_le_one`) and the partner factor `≤ 1`; summing the uniform per-edge bound over the shell (`Finset.sum_le_card_nsmul`) gives the geometric `cf^{(k+1−R)/(r₀+2)}` times the shell cardinality. With the cardinality polynomial in `k` and `cf < 1`, the per-stage increment `c_{k+1} − c_k` is summable | `CubicShellDecaySum.lean` | Per-stage increment (former Issue #2965) |
| `cubic_shell_card_le` / `derivBoundTight_cubic_shell_le_poly_pow` | **Polynomial × geometric shell bound (Phase B).** `cubic_shell_card_le`: the cut-edge shell of the `box_k`-slice has `≤ d·(2(k+1)+1)^d` edges (`Finset.card_filter_le` + handshake `inducedLatticeGraph_card_edgeFinset_le` + `card_cubicBox`). Combined with the geometric shell bound, `derivBoundTight_cubic_shell_le_poly_pow`: `derivBoundTight (inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) box_{k+1}) shell … ≤ β·J·(2·d·(2(k+1)+1)^d·cf^{(k+1−R)/(r₀+2)})` — the `M·(2k+3)^d·ratio^k` shape required by the volume-convergence-rate capstone | `CubicShellDecaySum.lean` | Per-stage increment (former Issue #2965) |

The direct composition was enabled by giving the broad fallback `Fintype edgeSet` instances in `CubicPerStageIncrement`/`CubicShellInfiniteVolumeBound` lower priority than the canonical `inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) Λ` edge-set instance (`LatticeBoundaryBED`), so both shell terms use the shared canonical instance. | `FunctionTheory.vitaliPorter_tendstoLocallyUniformlyOn` (proved in PR #4280) + `Ambient.correlationComplexAlongExhaustion_analytic_limit_of_volume_uniform` | **Vitali–Porter function-theory theorem + its correlation consumer (GJ §18.6/§18.7, Issue #4230 PR 3)** — `IsingModel/ComplexAnalyticity/FunctionTheoryAxioms.lean`, `AmbientComplexAnalyticity/Vitali/CorrelationVitaliPorter.lean`. `vitaliPorter_tendstoLocallyUniformlyOn` — the classical **Vitali–Porter convergence theorem** (formerly absent from Mathlib): for `U ⊆ ℂ` open and **preconnected**, a sequence `F : ℕ → ℂ → ℂ` of functions holomorphic on `U`, **locally uniformly bounded** on `U`, converging pointwise on a subset `S ⊆ U` with an accumulation point `z₀ ∈ U` (`AccPt z₀ (𝓟 S)`), converges **locally uniformly** on `U` to a holomorphic limit `f` agreeing with the pointwise limit on `S`. **This was the project's single non-Ising axiom; it is now proved from Mathlib** (`VitaliPorter/Theorem.lean`, PR #4280). `correlationComplexAlongExhaustion_analytic_limit_of_volume_uniform` applies it to the per-stage complex correlations: from the partition non-vanishing on `U` (per-stage holomorphicity, #4232), a **volume-uniform bound** on the correlations on `U` (the Ising-side cluster-expansion input, proven separately in later PRs), and pointwise convergence on `S` (supplied on the real axis by `correlationComplexAlongExhaustion_tendsto_at_real`), it yields `∃ f, DifferentiableOn ℂ f U ∧ TendstoLocallyUniformlyOn … f … ∧ Set.EqOn f g S` — exactly the `hconv` consumed by `_vitali_identified_at_real_of_ne_zero`. `#print axioms` on the consumer is `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]` (Vitali–Porter now proved). Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.6–18.7; Conway, *Functions of One Complex Variable I*, VII §2–3 (Montel/Vitali). | `IsingModel/ComplexAnalyticity/FunctionTheoryAxioms.lean`, `AmbientComplexAnalyticity/Vitali/CorrelationVitaliPorter.lean` | ∞-vol correlation Vitali (function-theory theorem + consumer; Issue #4230 PR 3) |
| `Ambient.correlationComplexAlongExhaustion_analytic_of_volume_uniform_bound` | **Infinite-volume correlation analyticity from a volume-uniform bound — real-axis Vitali application (GJ §18.6/§18.7, Issue #4230 PR 4)** — `AmbientComplexAnalyticity/Vitali/CorrelationRealAxisVitali.lean`. **Ising-side, fully proven**: builds the real-axis inputs to the Vitali–Porter consumer, reducing the infinite-volume correlation analyticity to the *then-single* remaining Ising hypothesis (the volume-uniform bound). For ferromagnetic `p`, on an open preconnected `U ∋ (p.β : ℂ)` where the along-exhaustion complex partition function is nonvanishing (`hZ`) and the per-stage complex correlations are volume-uniformly bounded (`hbdd`), the per-stage correlations converge **locally uniformly** on `U` to a holomorphic `f` with `f (p.β) = ↑(correlationInfinite G Λ p A)`. Proof: from `IsOpen U` (`Metric.isOpen_iff`) take a positive real segment `S = ofReal '' (p.β−δ, p.β+δ) ⊆ U` (half-width `δ = min ε p.β / 2`); each interior point is a ferromagnetic real parameter, so `correlationComplexAlongExhaustion_tendsto_at_real` gives pointwise convergence on `S` to `↑(correlationInfinite …)`, and `(p.β : ℂ)` is an accumulation point of `S` (`accPt_iff_nhds`; `Complex.isometry_ofReal.dist_eq`, `Complex.ofReal_inj`); the Vitali–Porter consumer (`_analytic_limit_of_volume_uniform`) then yields the locally uniform holomorphic limit, identified at `p.β` via `_vitali_identified_at_real_of_ne_zero` (#4232). `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]` — the Vitali–Porter theorem is now proved (#4280), so this is axiom-free; **all Ising content is proven**. Historical stage note: `hZ` and `hbdd` still had to be discharged here; the subsequent rows perform that discharge. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.6–18.7. | `AmbientComplexAnalyticity/Vitali/CorrelationRealAxisVitali.lean` | ∞-vol correlation Vitali (real-axis application; Issue #4230 PR 4) |
| `Ambient.volumeUniformZComplexIdentity_of_forall` | **Volume-uniform complex `Z` polymer identity (GJ §18.4–18.6, Issue #4230 PR 5)** — `AmbientComplexAnalyticity/VolumeUniformZIdentity.lean`. **Ising-side, fully proven, axiom-free** (`#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`). Discharges the structural input `VolumeUniformZComplexIdentity G Λ J` of the volume-uniform `hZ` provider (`VolumeUniformHZ.lean`) — the easier half of the volume-uniform complex partition non-vanishing. The complex high-temperature polymer factorization `Z_ℂ = 2^{|V_n|}·cosh(βJ)^{|E_n|}·∑_Γ ∏_P tanh(βJ)^{|P|}` holds, per fixed volume, on the disc where `Complex.cosh (βJ) ≠ 0` (the per-volume `partitionFunctionComplex_high_temp_expansion_h_zero_polymer_family_near_zero_beta`); **that disc is `n`-independent** (the zeros of `cosh(βJ)` depend only on `β, J`, not on the volume), so a *single* radius `r = ρ/2` works for **every** exhaustion stage. Proof: pick the `n`-independent `cosh`-disc `ball 0 ρ` (`Complex.continuous_cosh` + `Continuous.eventually_ne` + `Metric.eventually_nhds_iff_ball`); per stage `n`, both sides are analytic on it (`partitionFunctionComplex_analyticAt_beta`; `Complex.analyticOnNhd_cosh` + `vdPolymerFamilies_sum_tanh_analyticAt_complex_beta`), and the per-volume identity gives eventual agreement near `0`, so `AnalyticOnNhd.eqOn_of_preconnected_of_eventuallyEq` extends it to the whole disc (`convex_ball` preconnected; `Fintype.card_coe` bridges `Fintype.card ↑(Λ.volume n) = (Λ.volume n).card`). Combined with the lower bound `VolumeUniformComplexHTBound` (subsequent PR recorded below) this discharges, via `volume_uniform_Z_ne_zero_of_HT_bound_and_identity`, the volume-uniform partition non-vanishing `hZ` of #4235. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–18.6. | `AmbientComplexAnalyticity/VolumeUniformZIdentity.lean` | ∞-vol correlation Vitali (volume-uniform Z identity; Issue #4230 PR 5) |
| `vdPolymerFamilies_sum_pow_eq_exp_tsum_mayerExpansionTermComplex` | **Complex Mayer–Montroll log identity (GJ §18.4–18.6, Issue #4230 PR 5b)** — `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/ComplexMayerMontroll.lean`. **Ising-side, fully proven, axiom-free.** The complex-activity form of the Mayer–Montroll identity: on the Kotecký–Preiss ball `Metric.ball 0 R` (`(Δ(G))²·e·R < 1` + second KP condition), the polymer partition function equals the exponential of the complex cluster sum, `∑_Γ ∏_{P∈Γ} z^{|P|} = exp(∑_n mayerExpansionTermComplex G n z)`. Proven by **analytic continuation** from the real Mayer–Montroll (`polymerFreeEnergy_eq_tsum_mayerExpansionTerm_of_persite_kp`): the LHS is a polynomial (analytic), the RHS is `exp` of the analytic complex cluster sum (`mayerExpansionTermComplex_tsum_differentiableOn_ball` + `DifferentiableOn.analyticOnNhd` + `AnalyticAt.cexp`), and they agree at the real points `1/(k+1) → 0` (where the real polymer sum is `exp(polymerFreeEnergy) = exp(∑ mayerExpansionTerm)` via `vdPolymerFamilies_sum_pos_of_nonneg` + `Real.exp_log`, with casts `Complex.ofReal_exp`/`ofReal_tsum`/`mayerExpansionTermComplex_ofReal`), so the identity theorem `AnalyticOnNhd.eqOn_of_preconnected_of_frequently_eq` extends the identity to the whole ball. This is the key ingredient for the volume-uniform lower bound `VolumeUniformComplexHTBound`: it gives `‖∑_Γ ∏ tanh(βJ)^{|P|}‖ = exp(Re ∑ mayer) ≥ exp(−|Λ|·kpBound)`, whence (with the prefactor and bounded edge density) the volume-uniform partition non-vanishing `hZ` of #4235. `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.4–18.6. | `ClusterExpansion/MayerCore/ComplexMayerMontroll.lean` | ∞-vol correlation Vitali (complex Mayer–Montroll; Issue #4230 PR 5b) |

So the two-point ratio `Q_{i,j}/Q_∅` is the `s²`-coefficient ratio — the entry point for the source-marked Mayer/cluster expansion (later bricks) and the volume-uniform anchored bound. `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. Carrying `L` abstractly **avoids the branch cut of the principal `Complex.log`** (which would obstruct differentiability when `Q_∅` is on the negative real axis); the actual `L` is supplied by the source-marked Mayer expansion (later bricks). `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. Proof avoids the equality bijection (no surjectivity/right-inverse): `‖∑_{X:∂X={i,j}} t^{|X|}‖ ≤ ∑_X ‖t‖^{|X|}` (`norm_sum_le`), then the **injective** forward map `X ↦ (connectingComponentOf X, X∖C)` (left-inverse `C∪(X∖C)=X`; `X∖C` is even since every other component has empty boundary, `oddBoundary_eq_empty_of_mem_erase_connectingComponent`; `C⊥X∖C` by vertex-disjointness; `|X|=|C|+|X∖C|`) gives `∑_X ‖t‖^{|X|} = ∑_{image} ≤ ∑_{connectingPairs}` (`Finset.sum_image` + `sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg`), and the pair sum factors over the filtered product. (3) `sum_le_geometric_of_fiber_card_le`/`sum_le_geometric_closed_of_fiber_card_le`: pure geometric fiber-count summation (weight `≤A·a^{size}`, fiber card `≤B^n` ⟹ sum `≤A/(1-aB)` for `aB<1`). **Headline** `twoPointRatio_norm_le_geometric`: given the per-component bound `‖t‖^{|C|}·‖ZAvoid_C/Q_∅‖ ≤ A·a^{|C|}` and `a·Δ²<1`, then `‖Q_{i,j}/Q_∅‖ ≤ A/(1-a·Δ²)` — volume-uniform. At this historical stage the remaining hypothesis was the **local KP avoiding-ratio estimate** `‖ZAvoid_C(t)/Q_∅(t)‖ ≤ exp(κ·|support C|)` (the genuine cluster-expansion core; `A=exp κ`, `a=‖t‖·exp κ`); subsequent rows discharge it. `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. Now `Q_∅ = exp(M_G)`, `ZAvoid_C = exp(M_{Gavoid})` both via the same Mayer–Montroll, and `M_G − M_{Gavoid}` is the touching-cluster difference (subsequent rows: the rooted fixed-vertex KP bound). `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. (1) `norm_mayerExpansionTermComplex_tsum_sub_Gavoid_le_support_card_complex`: the **complex-`z`** generalization of the local KP avoiding-ratio estimate (#4252) — `‖(∑'_n mayerExpansionTermComplex G n z) − (∑'_n mayerExpansionTermComplex (Gavoid G C) n z)‖ ≤ κ(‖z‖)·(polymerSupport C).card` for any `z : ℂ` with `Δ²·e·‖z‖` in the KP range (the per-order chain only needs `‖z‖`; needed because the capstone evaluates at `z = tanh(βJ)`, complex for complex `β`). (2) `polymerSupport_card_le_card_add_one_of_isEdgeConnected`: for a nonempty edge-connected `C ⊆ G.edgeFinset`, `|polymerSupport C| ≤ |C| + 1` (a connected graph on its support has `|V| ≤ |E|+1`). Together these supply the complex per-component bound `‖t‖^{|C|}·‖ZAvoid_C/Q_∅‖ ≤ A·a^{|C|}` (`A=exp κ`, `a=‖t‖·exp κ`, via (1)+(2)+#4247) and the correlation↔ratio identity (3) needed to feed `twoPointRatio_norm_le_geometric` (#4244) and obtain the volume-uniform correlation bound (the subsequent capstone rows). `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. | `correlationComplex_two_point_norm_le_of_high_temp` | **Finite-graph high-temperature two-point correlation bound (degree-uniform value) — route B brick 5c.6-3 (GJ §18.6–18.7, FV §3.7.3, Issue #4230 PR 21)** — `ClusterExpansion/TwoPointCorrelationHTBound.lean`. **Ising-side, fully proven, axiom-free.** The per-graph input to the infinite-volume `hbdd` capstone: for any finite `G` with `G.maxDegree ≤ Δ`, there is a small β-disc on which `‖correlationComplex G {i,j} (J:ℂ) 0 β‖ ≤ twoPointHTBoundValue Δ`, where `twoPointHTBoundValue Δ = exp 8 / (1 − twoPointHTActivityRadius Δ · exp 8 · Δ²)` depends **only on `Δ`** (explicit in the statement) — so the same value works for all graphs of bounded degree (essential for applying it to every exhaustion stage). Assembles the chain: at `t = tanh(βJ)` on the disc, the per-component bound `‖t‖^{|C|}·‖ZAvoid_C/Q_∅‖ ≤ exp 8·(‖t‖·exp 8)^{|C|}` (from the complex KP bound #4253 with a coarse `κ ≤ 8` for `rr < 1/64` (the `1/6` threshold is for the KP tail condition), #4247, and `|support C| ≤ |C|+1` #4253) feeds `twoPointRatio_norm_le_geometric` (#4244) → `‖Q_{i,j}/Q_∅‖ ≤ twoPointHTBoundValue Δ`; the public tanh bridge (#4253) converts to `correlationComplex`. The β-disc is the intersection of the degree-uniform activity disc (`‖tanh(βJ)‖ < R` by continuity, `tanh 0 = 0`) with #4239's expansion radius. The subsequent capstone row applies this to the cubic exhaustion (`Δ = 2d` uniform) and feeds `correlationComplexAlongExhaustion_analytic_of_volume_uniform_bound` (#4235). `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. References: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.6–18.7; Friedli–Velenik, §3.7.3. | `ClusterExpansion/TwoPointCorrelationHTBound.lean` | ∞-vol correlation Vitali (finite-graph two-point HT bound; Issue #4230 PR 21) |
| `Ambient.correlationInfinite_latticeGraph_two_point_analytic_high_temp` | **Infinite-volume high-temperature analyticity of the two-point function — ROUTE B FINAL CAPSTONE (GJ §18.6–18.7, FV §3.7.3, Issue #4230 item D)** — `ClusterExpansion/TwoPointCorrelationInfiniteAnalytic.lean`. **Now FULLY axiom-free**: the Vitali–Porter theorem it rests on is proved from Mathlib (`VitaliPorter/Theorem.lean`, #4280), so this item-D capstone no longer depends on any declared axiom. For the cubic lattice `latticeGraph d`, any exhaustion `Λ` of `ℤ^d`, `J ≥ 0`, and `i ≠ j`, there is a radius `r > 0` such that for every small real `β ∈ (0, r)` the along-exhaustion complex two-point correlations `n ↦ correlationComplexAlongExhaustion (latticeGraph d) Λ {i,j} (J:ℂ) 0 z n` (complex variable = the inverse temperature, with `J` fixed real) converge locally uniformly on `Metric.ball 0 r` to a function `f` that is `DifferentiableOn ℂ` there and satisfies `f(β) = correlationInfinite (latticeGraph d) Λ ⟨J,0,β⟩ {i,j}` at that real `β` — i.e. at high temperature the infinite-volume two-point function arises as a `DifferentiableOn ℂ` locally-uniform limit of the finite-volume complex correlations in the complex inverse temperature. **Layer 1** re-derives the #4239 ratio identity `correlationComplex = htSubgraphSum {i,j} / htSubgraphSum ∅` on a degree-uniform β-disc `twoPointHTUniformRadius (2d) J` (intersection of the graph-independent `cosh(βJ) ≠ 0` disc with the Δ-uniform activity disc `‖tanh(βJ)‖ < twoPointHTActivityRadius (2d)`), the denominator being nonzero via `htSubgraphSum_empty_eq_exp_tsum_mayerExpansionTermComplex` = `exp(M_G) ≠ 0`; bounding the ratio by `twoPointHTBoundValue (2d)` (#4254) gives `correlationComplexAlongExhaustion_two_point_norm_le_uniform`: a single disc on which **all** exhaustion stages are bounded by the same Δ-uniform value (`induced_latticeGraph_maxDegree_le` gives `Δ ≤ 2d` at every stage; uncovered stages are `0`). **Layer 2** feeds this `hbdd` together with the volume-uniform partition non-vanishing `hZ` (#4238) into `correlationComplexAlongExhaustion_analytic_of_volume_uniform_bound` (#4235), whose conclusion uses the proved Vitali–Porter theorem and is the headline. This completes Issue #4230 item D (infinite-volume two-point correlation analyticity at high temperature), now unconditionally: `#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]` (the Vitali–Porter axiom was discharged, #4280). References: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §18.6–18.7; Friedli–Velenik, §3.7.3. | `ClusterExpansion/TwoPointCorrelationInfiniteAnalytic.lean` | ∞-vol two-point correlation analyticity (FINAL capstone; Issue #4230 item D) |
| `htSubgraphSum_anchored_peel` + `htSubgraphSumAvoiding'` + `existsUnique_component_mem_oddBoundary` (+ cross-check `htSubgraphSum_anchored_peel_pair`) | **K1 — anchored one-component peel identity for the HT-expansion numerator (GJ Theorem 17.6.1, p.313; §18 cluster expansion §18.4–18.7, Issue #4404 PR 1)** — `ClusterExpansion/AnchoredPeel.lean`. **Ising-side, fully proven, axiom-free.** Generalizes the pair-boundary numerator factorization `htSubgraphSum_pair_eq_sum_connectingComponent` (earlier in this table) from a two-point boundary `{i,j}` to a **general nonempty even boundary set `A`** with anchor `a ∈ A`: grouping `Q_A(t) = htSubgraphSum G A t` by its unique anchored component (`existsUnique_component_mem_oddBoundary`, the general-`A` analogue of `existsUnique_component_oddBoundary_pair`) and that component's boundary block `B = ∂C`, `Q_A(t) = ∑_{B ∈ evenSubsetsThrough A a} ∑_{C ∈ connectedComponentsWithBoundary G B} t^{|C|} · Q^{av}_{C,A∖B}(t)`, where `Q^{av}_{C,A'} = htSubgraphSumAvoiding' G C A' t = ∑_{Y⊥C, ∂Y=A'} t^{|Y|}` is the boundary-`A'` generalization of `htSubgraphSumAvoiding` (recovered at `A'=∅`). The remainder boundary is genuine set difference `A∖B` (component boundaries are disjoint — no symmetric-difference cancellation), and `a∈B` forces `B≠∅`, hence `|A∖B|<|A|`, the strict decrease that later powers an induction on `|A|`. **Cross-check** `htSubgraphSum_anchored_peel_pair`: at `A={i,j}` (anchor `a=i`) the identity collapses to the existing `htSubgraphSum_pair_eq_sum_connectingComponent`. Hence `vectorWeight ≤ exp K · ∏ᵢ modSpinDensity b A (ξ i)`, a product of integrable modified single-spin densities `exp(b·|ξ|² − A·|ξ|⁴)` (`integrable_modSpinDensity`, via the radial bound `integrableOn_radial_quad_quartic` — completing the square `b·r² − A·r⁴ ≤ b²/(2A) − (A/2)r⁴` reduces to Mathlib's super-Gaussian); `Integrable.fintype_prod` and `Integrable.mono'` finish (`integrable_vectorWeight`). The partition function is then strictly positive (`vectorPartition_pos`, via `integral_pos_of_integrable_nonneg_nonzero`), so the Gibbs expectation `⟨·⟩` of Theorem 4.7.1 is well defined. | `ContinuousSpin/TwoComponentMultiIntegrable.lean` | GJ §4.7 Thm 4.7.1 (multi-site integrability + partition positivity), p. 70 |

The monomial-weighted density is integrable (`integrable_pow_mul_singleSpinDensity`): in polar coordinates the norm is `≤ r^{a+b+1}·exp(−A·r⁴ − σ·r²)`, whose radial integral is finite by `integrableOn_pow_quad_quartic` (complete the square, dominate by the super-Gaussian `r^n·exp(−(A/2)r⁴)`). This is the single-site building block of the first inequality `⟨t^A q^B⟩ ≥ 0` (the continuous-spin Griffiths-I development continues in subsequent PRs). | `ContinuousSpin/TwoComponentGriffiths.lean` | GJ §4.7 Thm 4.7.1 (Griffiths-I single-site core), p. 70 |

The §4.7 duplicate-variable rotation is the *block* `√2`-rotation given by the formulas (4.3.2) applied to the pair `(t,q)` and its duplicate `(t',q')`: `α = (t+t')/√2`, `β = (t−t')/√2`, `γ = (q+q')/√2`, `δ = (q'−q)/√2` — mixing the two copies coordinate by coordinate (the `t`-pair into `(α,β)`, the `q`-pair into `(γ,δ)`). The capstone `doubled_integral_nonneg`: if `(F(ξ)−F(ξ'))·(G(ξ)−G(ξ')) = dSpinEval obs (rotLin∘dCoord)` for a non-negative-coefficient `obs`, then `0 ≤ ∫∫ (F(ξ)−F(ξ'))(G(ξ)−G(ξ')) W(ξ) W(ξ')` — proved by the change of variables (`measurePreserving_combinedEquiv.integral_comp'`), the weight factorization (`vectorWeight_mul_eq_rot`), and the doubled-rotated cone non-negativity (`dRotInteraction_nonneg`). This is the engine of the second/third inequalities; combined with the GKS-II doubling consequence it yields the headline (4.7.6)–(4.7.8) once the difference-observable non-negative-coefficient expansion is supplied. | `ContinuousSpin/TwoComponentChangeOfVariables.lean` | GJ §4.7 Thm 4.7.1 (4.7.6)–(4.7.8) change of variables + doubled-integral engine, pp. 70–71 |
| `ContinuousSpin.plusProd`/`minusProd` (+ `nncoeffs_plusProd`/`nncoeffs_evenSum_oddDiff`/`nncoeffs_oddDiff`/`dSpinEval_plusProd`/`_minusProd`), `dSpinEval_mul`/`_C_mul_sub`/`_neg`, `vSpinT_eq_rot`/`vSpinT'_eq_rot`/`vSpinQ_eq_rot`/`vSpinQ'_eq_rot`, `vectorMonomial_{t,t',q,q'}_eq`, `tMon_diff`/`qMon_diff`, `vectorMonomial_*_eq_genMonomial`, `integrable_vm_*`, `vectorExpectation_neg`, **`vectorExpectation_t_mul_le`** (4.7.6), **`vectorExpectation_q_mul_le`** (4.7.7), **`vectorExpectation_t_mul_q_le`** (4.7.8), `vectorExpectation_{t,q,t_q}_pair_le` (Cor 4.7.2) | **The second/third inequalities of GJ Theorem 4.7.1 (Lebowitz for two-component spins) — COMPLETE** (4.7.6)–(4.7.8) + Cor 4.7.2, pp. 70–71; Issue #3918). The block rotation recovers the single-copy spins `tᵢ = (αᵢ+βᵢ)/√2`, `tᵢ' = (αᵢ−βᵢ)/√2`, `qᵢ = (γᵢ−δᵢ)/√2`, `qᵢ' = (γᵢ+δᵢ)/√2` (`vSpinT_eq_rot`, …), so the difference of a `t`- (resp. `q`-) monomial across the duplicate is `(√2/2)^{|A|}` times `plusProd − minusProd` on slots `0,1` (resp. `2,3`) (`tMon_diff`, `qMon_diff`).  The **odd difference `plusProd − minusProd` has non-negative coefficients** by mutual induction with the even sum (`nncoeffs_evenSum_oddDiff`), so the product of two difference observables is `dSpinEval` of a non-negative-coefficient polynomial; `doubled_integral_nonneg` then gives the doubled-integral positivity, and the GKS-II doubling consequence `vectorExpectation_mul_le_of_doubled_nonneg` yields `⟨t^A⟩⟨t^B⟩ ≤ ⟨t^A t^B⟩` (4.7.6, `vectorExpectation_t_mul_le`), `⟨q^A⟩⟨q^B⟩ ≤ ⟨q^A q^B⟩` (4.7.7, `vectorExpectation_q_mul_le`), and `⟨t^A q^B⟩ ≤ ⟨t^A⟩⟨q^B⟩` (4.7.8, `vectorExpectation_t_mul_q_le`, via the `−q^B` reflection and `vectorExpectation_neg`).  Integrability of the monomials against the Gibbs weight is supplied through the `genMonomial` bridge (`vectorMonomial_*_eq_genMonomial` + `integrable_genMonomial_mul_vectorWeight`).  The pairwise specializations (Cor 4.7.2) follow by `A = {i}`, `B = {j}`.  This completes Theorem 4.7.1. | `ContinuousSpin/TwoComponentLebowitz.lean` | GJ §4.7 Thm 4.7.1 (4.7.6)–(4.7.8) + Cor 4.7.2, pp. 70–71 |

**Status (as of PR #2729).** The
local-branch form of GJ Thm 4.6.2 finite-volume analyticity is
formalised: `exists_freeEnergyComplex_analyticAt_branch_of_leeYangDomain`
gives, at every `h₀ ∈ leeYangDomain`, an analytic function `f` with
`exp(|ι|·f(h₀)) = Z(h₀)` and `f(h₀) = freeEnergyComplex(h₀)`
(basepoint). Additionally, the Vitali bridge
`vitali_bridge_leeYangDomain` and all modulus bounds
(`norm_partitionFunctionComplex_le_*`,
`norm_freeEnergyComplex_le_trivial_bound`) are in place. The conditional
along-exhaustion Vitali assembly
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_vitali_bridge_leeYangDomain_identified_at_real`
now packages the final handoff from locally uniform convergence to
Lee-Yang holomorphicity and real-axis identification with
`freeEnergyInfinite`. The along-exhaustion per-stage branch wrappers
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_analyticBranch_leeYangDomain_all_stages`
and
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_analyticOnNhd_branch_ball_all_stages`
also package the finite-stage Lee-Yang local branches needed before a
future normal-family argument. The strong ball-level wrappers
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_analyticOnNhd_branch_ball_all_stages_strong`
add basepoint normalisation to the same local witness. The local branch-family
assembly
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_branchFamily_vitali_ball_identified_at_center`
now consumes a locally-uniformly convergent chosen branch family on a ball and
identifies the centre value with `↑freeEnergyInfinite`. The local-cover
handoff
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_branchFamily_vitali_localCover_real`
globalises this conditional statement: if every Lee-Yang point has a local
branch-family witness converging to the same `f`, then `f` is holomorphic on
`leeYangDomain`; under `BoundedEdgeDensity G Λ`,
`DisjointTowerHypotheses G Λ p`, and `(p.h : ℂ) ∈ leeYangDomain`, it also
agrees with `freeEnergyInfinite` at the real centre. PR #2693 adds the
corresponding subsequence-indexed handoff
`Ambient.freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_subseq_branchFamily_vitali_localCover_real`:
after a Montel-style extraction with a strictly increasing stage map `σ`, the
same local-cover Vitali and real-axis identification conclusions apply to
branch families whose identities are written at stage `σ m`. The compact-uniform
`Z_ℂ` input
`Ambient.norm_partitionFunctionComplexAlongExhaustion_le_on_isCompact_stage`
now supplies a single real-part bound for all `h` in a compact field set and
all stages of the exhaustion, with the numerical right-hand side still allowed
to depend on the stage. Under bounded edge density and nonvanishing, the upper
normalised-log bridge
`Ambient.exists_real_log_norm_partitionFunctionComplexAlongExhaustion_div_card_le_on_isCompact`
turns it into a stage-independent upper bound for
`Real.log ‖Z_{Λ_n}(h)‖ / |Λ_n|` on compact field sets. On compact subsets of
`leeYangDomain`, `Ambient.exists_log_norm_partitionFunctionComplexAlongExhaustion_div_card_le_leeYangDomain`
uses Lee-Yang nonvanishing to discharge that nonzero hypothesis under
`β > 0`, `J > 0`. This still provides only the upper side of the absolute-log
estimate. The normalised-log bridge
`Ambient.norm_freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_le_of_abs_log_norm_bound_on_set`
then records the conditional passage from a uniform
`|log ‖Z_{Λ_n}(h)‖| / |Λ_n|` bound to the corresponding principal
free-energy bound; deriving that normalised-log bound remains part of the
future normal-family construction. The stage-independent form
`Ambient.norm_freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_le_of_abs_log_norm_bound_on_set_uniform`
packages the resulting locally bounded family as `‖f_n(h)‖ ≤ C + π` on the
same set. PRs #2683-#2691 packaged the Lee-Yang upper/lower-log handoff, the
compact fugacity gap, finite-stage polynomial and `Z_ℂ` lower bounds, the
conditional polynomial-witness lower-log bridge, and compact/ball/point-local
conditional locally bounded wrappers. PR #2692 discharges that remaining
polynomial-witness lower normalised-log input on compact subsets of
`leeYangDomain`: it introduces the one-variable specialization
`MultilinPoly.uniformPolynomial`, proves the root-product estimate
`Polynomial.one_sub_radius_pow_natDegree_le_norm_eval_of_roots_norm_ge_one`,
and applies Lee-Yang nonvanishing to show that the specialized Ising
polynomial has constant value `q(0)=1` and all roots outside the open unit
disk. If the compact fugacity gap gives `‖exp(-2βh)‖ ≤ r < 1` on
`K ⊆ leeYangDomain`, the resulting witness is
`ε_n=(1-r)^|Λ_n|`, with `Real.log ε_n / |Λ_n| = Real.log (1-r)`. Thus
`Ambient.exists_poly_lower_norm_isingEdgePoly_eval_leeYangFugacityVec_on_isCompact`
and
`Ambient.exists_lower_log_norm_partitionFunctionComplexAlongExhaustion_leeYang_of_isCompact`
provide the stage-uniform lower normalised-log bound without an external
polynomial-witness hypothesis. The unconditional locally bounded family
wrappers
`Ambient.exists_norm_freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_le_leeYang_of_isCompact`,
`Ambient.exists_norm_freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_le_leeYang_on_ball`, and
`Ambient.exists_norm_freeEnergyComplexAlongExhaustion_le_leeYang_around`, plus
their ℤ^d wrappers in `PerStageComplex.lean`, now supply the local boundedness
input needed for the remaining normal-family/Vitali branch construction. PRs
#2694-#2696 package the compact-open extraction handoff on one ball, then on
two balls, then on a finite family of Lee-Yang balls, producing one common
strictly increasing subsequence and local differentiable limits under explicit
compact-open compactness hypotheses. PR #2697 adds the explicit-overlap
handoff: if the selected stage branches are eventually equal on every pairwise
overlap, then the extracted local limits agree pairwise on those overlaps. PR
#2698 adds the finite local-patching handoff, patching such compatible local
limits to one differentiable function on the finite union of balls. PR #2699
adds the open-cover patching handoff, replacing the finite-index restriction
with an arbitrary compatible open cover whose union covers `leeYangDomain`.
PR #2700 packages that open-cover handoff for the canonical pointed cover by
Lee-Yang balls indexed by the points of `leeYangDomain`. PR #2701 packages
each point-centred ball's branch-limit witness into
`Ambient.LeeYangLocalBranchLimit` and patches compatible point-indexed
structured data through the same pointed local-cover assembly. PR #2702 adds
the corresponding structured-data real-centre identification wrapper. PR
#2703 packages the data and compatibility into one
`Ambient.LeeYangLocalBranchLimitFamily` endpoint. PR #2704 adds the
real-centred package `Ambient.LeeYangRealBranchLimitFamily`, including
Lee-Yang membership of the real centre and centre normalisation. PR #2705
packages finite compact-open diagonal/overlap output as
`Ambient.LeeYangFiniteSubseqBranchLimitFamily`, with a patching wrapper for the
finite union of balls. PR #2706 adds the corresponding finite packaged
real-centre identification wrapper. PR #2707 composes the compact-open finite
package extraction directly with that real-centre patch. PR #2708 adds the
corresponding compact-open extraction-to-patch wrapper without the real-centre
identification conclusion. PR #2728 connects the pre-Montel
`LeeYangPointwiseNormalisedAllStageBranchData` package to that finite
compact-open extraction and finite patch endpoint for selected Lee-Yang
centres, still under explicit compact-open compactness and overlap-equality
hypotheses. PR #2709 packages finite Lee-Yang cover geometry,
and PR #2729 gives the direct all-stage-data finite Lee-Yang cover bridge using
the radii and Lee-Yang-domain containment already stored in the all-stage
package. PR #2730 adds the all-stage selected real-centre and compact-target
finite-cover patch wrappers, identifying the real-centre value with
`↑freeEnergyInfinite` on compact targets covered by the selected balls.
PR #2731 extracts the selected finite all-stage balls from compactness of
`K`, packages the real-centred `Fin n` geometry, and feeds that geometry back
into the PR #2730 compact real-cover patch bridge.
PR #2710 bundles the selected real-centre index, and PR #2711 adds the compact
target `K` covered by those finite balls.

**Not yet formalized** (future PRs):
- Montel-style locally uniform convergence/subsequence extraction of local
  Lee-Yang branch families from the now-unconditional local boundedness plus
  real-axis Fekete and the branch-family identity theorem; PRs #2694-#2711
  provide post-extraction and conditional patching handoffs through finite
  Lee-Yang covers, finite real-centred cover packages, compact finite targets, arbitrary open covers,
  the pointed Lee-Yang ball cover, and structured local-cover branch-limit
  data, but mathlib still lacks a direct Montel theorem.
- Coherent branch-family construction giving the explicit overlap hypothesis,
  and diagonal coherent local-cover extraction supplying the compatible
  open-cover input.
- Note: the principal-branch `freeEnergyComplex` may be discontinuous
  where `Z` crosses the negative real axis; the local-branch form
  is the mathematically correct statement of GJ Thm 4.6.2.
